Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a summary document for you:

## Summary: `render_chart` Tool Definition, Schema, and Invocation

Based on my thorough search of the codebase, here's a complete breakdown of the `render_chart` tool:

### 1. Tool Definition and Schema

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/mcp_sse.py` (lines 70-94)

The tool is defined in the MCP server's `list_tools()` handler:

```python
types.Tool(
    name="render_chart",
    description=(
        "Render a Plotly chart. Call this as the final step after querying data. "
        "Pass all Plotly traces in `data` and a layout object with a descriptive `title`."
    ),
    inputSchema={
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "data": {
                "type": "array",
                "description": "Array of Plotly trace objects (bar, line, scatter, pie, etc.)",
            },
            "layout": {
                "type": "object",
                "description": "Plotly layout object",
                "properties": {
                    "title": {"type": "string"},
                },
                "required": ["title"],
            },
        },
        "required": ["data", "layout"],
    },
)
```

**Required Input Properties:**
- `data` (array): Array of Plotly trace objects (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, box, heatmap, etc.)
- `layout` (object): Plotly layout object with required sub-property:
  - `title` (string): Descriptive chart title — **mandatory**

### 2. Tool Invocation Instructions for LLM

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 80-96)

The chart-builder subagent receives this prompt guidance:

```python
chart_prompt = (
    "You are a data visualization expert using Plotly. Given a user's request for "
    "a chart or visualization, query the data with SQL and then call the "
    "`render_chart` tool with the Plotly spec.\n\n"
    "Guidelines:\n"
    "- Choose the most appropriate chart type (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, "
    "box, heatmap, etc.).\n"
    "- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.\n"
    "- For multi-series data, group into separate traces.\n"
    "- `layout.title` is required — always provide a descriptive title.\n"
    "- Keep the chart clean and readable.\n\n"
    "When ready to render, call `render_chart` with:\n"
    "- `data`: array of Plotly trace objects\n"
    "- `layout`: object containing at minimum {\"title\": \"<descriptive title>\"}\n\n"
    "Do NOT output a JSON code block. Use the tool.\n"
    + table_schemas
)
```

**Key instruction:** The chart-builder is explicitly told to **call the tool, not emit a JSON code block**.

### 3. Tool Registration in Agent Definition

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 108-115)

The chart-builder agent definition includes the tool:

```python
"chart-builder": AgentDefinition(
    description=(
        "Use this agent when the user wants a chart, graph, or visualization."
    ),
    prompt=chart_prompt,
    tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql", "mcp__duckdb__render_chart"],
    model=CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL,
),
```

The tool is named `mcp__duckdb__render_chart` in the tools list.

### 4. Validation Logic

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/mcp_sse.py` (lines 129-138)

When the tool is called, the MCP handler validates the input:

```python
elif name == "render_chart":
    data = arguments.get("data")
    layout = arguments.get("layout", {})
    if not isinstance(data, list) or not layout.get("title"):
        logger.warning("render_chart called with missing data or layout.title: %s", arguments)
        return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "error", "error": "data and layout.title are required"}))]
    # Return a minimal acknowledgement. The chart spec (data + layout) is captured
    # upstream from the tool_use input block in the backend stream handler — not from
    # this response — so we don't need to echo the payload here.
    return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "rendered"}))]
```

**Validation checks:**
1. `data` must be a list
2. `layout.title` must be present (non-empty)
3. Returns error JSON if validation fails
4. Returns `{"status": "rendered"}` on success

### 5. Chart Spec Capture in Stream Handler

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 136-152)

The backend captures the tool input directly from the assistant message stream:

```python
def _build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages(
    msg: dict, tool_names: dict[str, str], subagent_chart_specs: dict
) -> None:
    """Inspect an assistant message and capture render_chart input as chart_spec.

    When the chart-builder subagent calls render_chart, the tool_use block
    appears in an assistant message whose parent_tool_use_id is the Task
    tool_use_id for the chart-builder.  We extract input directly so we never
    need to parse free-text JSON.
    """
    parent_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
    if not parent_id or tool_names.get(parent_id) != "chart-builder":
        return
    for block in msg.get("message", {}).get("content", []):
        if block.get("type") == "tool_use" and "render_chart" in block.get("name", ""):
            subagent_chart_specs[parent_id] = block.get("input", {})
            return
```

**Key insight:** The chart spec is extracted from the **tool_use.input** field in the assistant message, not from the tool result. This bypasses any parsing of free-text responses.

### 6. Test Coverage

Tests verify the tool's behavior in:
- **`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/tests/test_mcp_sse.py`** (lines 122-169):
  - Tool appears in MCP tool list
  - Schema requires `layout.title`
  - Tool call returns `{"status": "rendered"}`

- **`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/tests/test_stream_chart_capture.py`** (lines 23-69):
  - Input is captured from `render_chart` tool_use blocks
  - Tool calls from other subagents are ignored
  - Non-render_chart tool_use blocks are ignored

- **`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/tests/test_agent_chart.py`** (lines 17-33):
  - Chart-builder prompt instructs tool call (not JSON block)
  - Chart-builder tools include `render_chart`

### 7. Architecture Flow

```
chart-builder LLM (with render_chart tool available)
  ↓
Calls render_chart(data, layout) MCP tool
  ↓
Backend captures tool_use.input from assistant message
  ↓
chart_spec dict stored in subagent_chart_specs
  ↓
Emits "subagent_end" SSE event with chart_spec
  ↓
Frontend ChartWidget renders via Plotly
```

This replaces the previous regex-based `_extract_chart_spec()` approach with direct schema-validated input capture.

Here's the root cause analysis:

## Why `render_chart` input is often wrong

**The core problem is data volume in the tool call.** Looking at your example, the LLM is passing **hundreds of raw data points** per trace (e.g., 500+ values for 3rd Class alone). When the `tool_use` input becomes extremely large:

1. The LLM loses track of the JSON structure mid-generation
2. By the time it finishes writing the massive `data` array, it either truncates, malforms the JSON, or fails to include the `layout` property entirely
3. Result: `'layout' is a required property` — the `layout` key is simply missing from the output

This is a known LLM limitation — generating very long structured JSON outputs degrades structural accuracy.

**The fix:** The chart-builder prompt should instruct the LLM to **pre-aggregate data in SQL** rather than dumping raw values into the tool call. For example:

- **Box plots**: Compute `q1`, `median`, `q3`, `lowerfence`, `upperfence` in SQL and use Plotly's pre-computed box format instead of passing every raw value
- **Histograms**: Compute bin counts in SQL rather than passing raw values for Plotly to bin
- **Scatter/line with many points**: Sample or aggregate to keep data concise
- **General rule**: Keep each trace under ~50 data points

Want me to update the `chart_prompt` to include these data-reduction guidelines?

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me write a comprehensive report:

---

## Summary

The chart-builder subagent's answer text appears all at once in the UI rather than streaming incrementally because **subagent `text_delta` stream events are deliberately filtered out at the backend and accumulated instead of being streamed**.

The subagent's "answer" text only arrives as a **complete, non-streaming chunk** in the `subagent_end` SSE event, which is sent once per subagent after the subagent finishes. This is by design but creates the appearance that text is not streaming.

---

## Root Cause Analysis

### 1. Backend Filtering of Subagent Text Deltas (`backend/app/agent.py`, lines 332-343)

```python
elif delta_type == "text_delta":
    text = delta.get("text", "")
    if text:
        # Accumulate subagent text for chart_spec extraction
        if is_subagent_event:
            subagent_texts[stream_parent] = subagent_texts.get(stream_parent, "") + text
        else:
            event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
            yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
```

**Key Issue**: 
- When `is_subagent_event` is True (i.e., the text comes from a subagent as indicated by `parent_tool_use_id`), the backend **accumulates the text in memory** (`subagent_texts[stream_parent]`) instead of emitting it as streaming deltas
- For orchestrator text (`is_subagent_event` is False), it **immediately yields** `text_delta` events as "answer" or "thinking" events

### 2. Complete Assistant Messages Also Accumulated (`backend/app/agent.py`, lines 367-373)

```python
if parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in tool_names:
    text_parts = []
    for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
        if block.get("type") == "text" and block.get("text"):
            text_parts.append(block["text"])
    if text_parts:
        subagent_texts[parent_tool_use_id] = "\n".join(text_parts)
```

When an intermediate assistant message arrives for a subagent, its text is captured and stored in `subagent_texts[tool_id]`, replacing any previously accumulated deltas.

### 3. Single Bulk Emission on Subagent End (`backend/app/agent.py`, lines 469-489)

```python
if name in ("sql-analyst", "chart-builder"):
    if name == "chart-builder":
        chart_spec = subagent_chart_specs.get(tool_id)
        end_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
        if chart_spec:
            end_data["chart_spec"] = chart_spec
        else:
            end_data["result"] = tool_use_result_text or subagent_texts.get(tool_id, text)
        yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
```

The backend emits a **single** `subagent_end` SSE event containing:
- For chart-builder: the `chart_spec` (if rendered) or accumulated `result` text
- The text is bundled as one complete event, not streamed in chunks

### 4. Frontend Receives Complete Text in One Event (`frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`, lines 223-229)

```typescript
case 'subagent_end':
  callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
    id: data.id as string,
    name: data.name as string,
    result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
    chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
  });
  break;
```

The frontend's SSE handler invokes `onSubagentEnd` callback with the complete `result` text in one chunk.

### 5. Frontend Context Updates Message State Once (`frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`, lines 199-214)

```typescript
onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
  segmentsRef.current.push({
    type: 'subagent_end',
    subagentId: data.id,
    subagentName: data.name,
    chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
    text: data.result,  // Complete text, not streamed
  });
  setMessages((prev) =>
    prev.map((m) =>
      m.id === assistantId
        ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
        : m
    }
  );
};
```

The context pushes one `subagent_end` segment with the complete non-streaming text and updates React state once.

### 6. Rendering Displays Complete Text Immediately (`frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx`, lines 186-192)

```typescript
const chartSegments = hasSegments
  ? message.segments!.filter(
      (s) =>
        (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) ||
        (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
    )
  : [];
```

Chart segments (which may include the text in `subagent_end` segments) are rendered after the chart is displayed, but since the text was received as one complete chunk, it appears all at once.

---

## Data Flow Diagram

```
Subagent (TypeScript SDK in sidecar) sends:
  text_delta stream events → "This is the answer"
                              "about the chart"
                              
Backend (Python agent.py) processes:
  text_delta (is_subagent_event=true)
    → ACCUMULATES in subagent_texts dict (line 340)
    → NO SSE event emitted
    
  text_delta (orchestrator)
    → IMMEDIATELY yields event: answer/thinking
  
  Complete assistant message from subagent
    → Captures full text in subagent_texts (line 373)
  
  Tool result for subagent
    → Emits ONE subagent_end event with complete text (line 484)

Frontend (React) receives:
  event: subagent_end
  data: {"id": "...", "name": "chart-builder", "result": "complete text", ...}
    → onSubagentEnd callback fires ONCE
    → One segment added to segmentsRef
    → One setMessages update

UI renders:
  Display receives complete "result" text
  → Text appears instantly, no streaming animation
```

---

## Why This Design?

The backend deliberately suppresses streaming of subagent text deltas because:

1. **Chart extraction requires complete text**: The subagent's text may contain post-thinking explanation. The backend needs the full text to extract `chart_spec` from the `render_chart` tool use.

2. **Tool result handling**: The SDK doesn't yield the subagent's final assistant message to the orchestrator. Instead, the tool_use_result metadata contains only `{agentId, usage}`. The actual text lives in intermediate assistant messages (`parent_tool_use_id` set). Accumulating and emitting in `subagent_end` ensures the text isn't lost.

3. **Prevents duplicate/interleaved output**: Without filtering, subagent text deltas would appear in the thinking section, and the complete text might be duplicated elsewhere.

---

## Key Files

1. **Backend streaming logic**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 318-489)
   - Lines 332-343: Filter and accumulate subagent text deltas
   - Lines 367-373: Capture complete assistant messages
   - Lines 469-489: Emit single `subagent_end` event

2. **Frontend SSE parsing**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts` (lines 175-230)
   - Lines 223-229: Handle `subagent_end` event

3. **Frontend state management**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` (lines 199-214)
   - Receives complete text, pushes one segment

4. **Frontend rendering**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx` (lines 186-192)
   - Renders complete text without streaming animation

---

## To Enable Streaming of Subagent Text

To make the chart-builder's answer text stream incrementally, you would need to:

1. **Stream subagent text deltas to frontend** by emitting SSE events for each `text_delta` when `is_subagent_event=true`
2. **Add a new event type** like `subagent_text` to distinguish from orchestrator text
3. **Update frontend handler** to accumulate and render these deltas progressively
4. **Defer chart_spec extraction** to use complete message from subagent end event (already done via `_build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages` at line 374)

This would require changes to `backend/app/agent.py`, `frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`, and `frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`.

   330→                                if text and not is_subagent_event:
   331→                                    yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   332→                            elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   333→                                text = delta.get("text", "")
   334→                                if text:
   335→                                    # Accumulate subagent text for chart_spec
   336→                                    # extraction (the SDK may not yield a
   337→                                    # complete assistant message for the
   338→                                    # subagent's final turn).
   339→                                    if is_subagent_event:
   340→                                        subagent_texts[stream_parent] = subagent_texts.get(stream_parent, "") + text
   341→                                    else:
   342→                                        event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
   343→                                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   344→
   345→                        elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   346→                            block = event.get("content_block", {})
   347→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   348→                            if block_type == "thinking":
   349→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   350→                                    has_thinking = True
   351→                            elif block_type == "text":
   352→                                if has_thinking and not is_subagent_event:
   353→                                    yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   354→                            elif block_type == "tool_use":
   355→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   356→                                    has_thinking = False
   357→                                    has_tool_calls = True
   358→
   359→                    # --- Complete assistant message (contains tool_use blocks) ---
   360→                    elif msg_type == "assistant":
   361→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   362→                        parent_tool_use_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   363→
   364→                        # Capture text from subagent assistant messages.
   365→                        # These carry the real subagent output (e.g. chart JSON)
   366→                        # that the Task tool_result metadata lacks.
   367→                        if parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in tool_names:
   368→                            text_parts = []
   369→                            for block in message_obj.get("content", []):

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   105→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   106→  userSessionId?: string,
   107→): Promise<void> {
   108→  try {
   109→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   110→      method: 'POST',
   111→      headers: {
   112→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   113→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   114→      },
   115→      body: JSON.stringify({
   116→        message,
   117→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   118→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   119→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   120→      }),
   121→      signal,
   122→    });
   123→
   124→    if (!response.ok) {
   125→      const errorText = await response.text();
   126→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   127→      return;
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   131→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   132→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   133→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   134→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   135→  }
   136→}
   137→
   138→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   139→  newMessage: string,
   140→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   141→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   142→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   143→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   144→  userSessionId?: string,
   145→): Promise<void> {
   146→  try {
   147→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {
   148→      method: 'POST',
   149→      headers: {
   150→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   151→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   152→      },
   153→      body: JSON.stringify({
   154→        new_message: newMessage,
   155→        conversation_history: conversationHistory,
   156→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   157→      }),
   158→      signal,
   159→    });
   160→
   161→    if (!response.ok) {
   162→      const errorText = await response.text();
   163→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   164→      return;
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   168→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   169→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   170→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   171→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   172→  }
   173→}
   174→
   175→function handleSSEEvent(
   176→  eventType: string,
   177→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   178→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   179→) {
   180→  switch (eventType) {
   181→    case 'thinking':
   182→    case 'answer':
   183→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   184→      break;
   185→    case 'thinking_done':
   186→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   187→      break;
   188→    case 'tool_call':
   189→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   190→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   191→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   192→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   193→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   194→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   195→        columns: [],
   196→        rows: [],
   197→        rowCount: 0,
   198→      });
   199→      break;
   200→    case 'tool_result': {
   201→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   202→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   203→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   204→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   205→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   206→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   207→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   208→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   209→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   210→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   211→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   212→      };
   213→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   214→      break;
   215→    }
   216→    case 'subagent_start':
   217→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   218→        id: data.id as string,
   219→        name: data.name as string,
   220→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   221→      });
   222→      break;
   223→    case 'subagent_end':
   224→      callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   225→        id: data.id as string,
   226→        name: data.name as string,
   227→        result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   228→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   229→      });
   230→      break;
   231→    case 'user_question': {
   232→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   233→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   234→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   235→        (opt: unknown) => {
   236→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   237→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   238→          return { label: String(opt) };
   239→        }
   240→      );
   241→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   242→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   243→        question: data.question as string,
   244→        options: normalizedOptions,
   245→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   246→      });
   247→      break;
   248→    }
   249→    case 'done':
   250→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   251→      break;
   252→    case 'error':
   253→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   254→      break;
   255→  }
   256→}
   257→

     1→import {
     2→  useCallback,
     3→  useRef,
     4→  useState,
     5→  type ReactNode,
     6→} from 'react';
     7→import { AgentContext } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     8→import { runAgentLoop, runAgentEditLoop } from '../agent/agentService';
     9→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, ToolCallResult } from '../types';
    10→import { useSessionId } from '../hooks/useSessionId';
    11→
    12→function generateId() {
    13→  return crypto.randomUUID();
    14→}
    15→
    16→export function AgentProvider({
    17→  children,
    18→  refreshTables,
    19→}: {
    20→  children: ReactNode;
    21→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    22→}) {
    23→  const userSessionId = useSessionId();
    24→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    25→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    26→  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
    27→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');
    28→  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    29→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');
    30→  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);
    31→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    32→  const phaseRef = useRef<'thinking' | 'answer'>('thinking');
    33→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    34→  const pendingHistoryRef = useRef<{ role: string; content: string }[] | null>(null);
    35→
    36→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    37→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    38→    if (!text) return;
    39→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    40→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    41→    setMessages((prev) =>
    42→      prev.map((m) =>
    43→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    44→      )
    45→    );
    46→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    47→  }, []);
    48→
    49→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    50→    async (text: string) => {
    51→      if (isStreaming) return;
    52→
    53→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    54→        id: generateId(),
    55→        role: 'user',
    56→        content: text,
    57→      };
    58→
    59→      const assistantId = generateId();
    60→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    61→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    62→        id: assistantId,
    63→        role: 'assistant',
    64→        content: '',
    65→        toolCalls: [],
    66→        isStreaming: true,
    67→      };
    68→
    69→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    70→      setIsStreaming(true);
    71→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    72→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    73→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    74→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
    75→
    76→      // If there's pending history from a delete, start a new Langfuse session with that context
    77→      const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
    78→      pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
    79→      const langfuseSessionId = pendingHistory ? crypto.randomUUID() : null;
    80→
    81→      const controller = new AbortController();
    82→      abortRef.current = controller;
    83→
    84→      await runAgentLoop(
    85→        text,
    86→        sessionIdRef.current,
    87→        langfuseSessionId,
    88→        pendingHistory,
    89→        {
    90→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
    91→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
    92→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
    93→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    94→                flushText();
    95→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
    96→              }, 50);
    97→            }
    98→          },
    99→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   100→            // Extended thinking just ended and a text block is starting.
   101→            // Create a thinking segment from accumulated thinking text.
   102→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   103→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   104→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   105→            }
   106→            flushText();
   107→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   108→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   109→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   110→            }
   111→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   112→            setMessages((prev) =>
   113→              prev.map((m) =>
   114→                m.id === assistantId
   115→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   116→                  : m
   117→              )
   118→            );
   119→          },
   120→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   121→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   122→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   123→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   124→            }
   125→            flushText();
   126→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   127→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   128→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   129→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   130→            }
   131→            // Add a pending tool segment so the input is shown immediately
   132→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   133→              type: 'tool',
   134→              toolResult: pending,
   135→            });
   136→            setMessages((prev) =>
   137→              prev.map((m) =>
   138→                m.id === assistantId
   139→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   140→                  : m
   141→              )
   142→            );
   143→          },
   144→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   145→            // Merge result into pending tool segment (keep input info, add output)
   146→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   147→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   148→            );
   149→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   150→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   151→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   152→                type: 'tool',
   153→                toolResult: {
   154→                  ...pending,
   155→                  ...result,
   156→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   157→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   158→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   159→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   160→                },
   161→              };
   162→            } else {
   163→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   164→            }
   165→            setMessages((prev) =>
   166→              prev.map((m) =>
   167→                m.id === assistantId
   168→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   169→                  : m
   170→              )
   171→            );
   172→            refreshTables();
   173→          },
   174→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   175→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   176→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   177→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   178→            }
   179→            flushText();
   180→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   181→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   182→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   183→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   184→            }
   185→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   186→              type: 'subagent_start',
   187→              subagentId: data.id,
   188→              subagentName: data.name,
   189→              text: data.prompt,
   190→            });
   191→            setMessages((prev) =>
   192→              prev.map((m) =>
   193→                m.id === assistantId
   194→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   195→                  : m
   196→              )
   197→            );
   198→          },
   199→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   200→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   201→              type: 'subagent_end',
   202→              subagentId: data.id,
   203→              subagentName: data.name,
   204→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   205→              text: data.result,
   206→            });
   207→            setMessages((prev) =>
   208→              prev.map((m) =>
   209→                m.id === assistantId
   210→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   211→                  : m
   212→              )
   213→            );
   214→          },
   215→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   216→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   217→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   218→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   219→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   220→            }
   221→            flushText();
   222→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   223→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   224→                type: 'answer',
   225→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   226→              });
   227→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   228→            }
   229→            setMessages((prev) =>
   230→              prev.map((m) =>
   231→                m.id === assistantId
   232→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   233→                  : m
   234→              )
   235→            );
   236→            setIsStreaming(false);
   237→            abortRef.current = null;
   238→          },
   239→          onError: (error) => {
   240→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   241→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   242→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   243→            }
   244→            flushText();
   245→            setMessages((prev) =>
   246→              prev.map((m) =>
   247→                m.id === assistantId
   248→                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\n\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }
   249→                  : m
   250→              )
   251→            );
   252→            setIsStreaming(false);
   253→            abortRef.current = null;
   254→          },
   255→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   256→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   257→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   258→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   259→            }
   260→            flushText();
   261→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   262→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   263→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   264→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   265→            }
   266→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   267→              type: 'user_question',
   268→              questionData: data,
   269→            });
   270→            setMessages((prev) =>
   271→              prev.map((m) =>
   272→                m.id === assistantId
   273→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   274→                  : m
   275→              )
   276→            );
   277→          },
   278→        },
   279→        controller.signal,
   280→        userSessionId,
   281→      );
   282→    },
   283→    [isStreaming, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   284→  );
   285→
   286→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   287→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {
   288→      if (isStreaming) return;
   289→
   290→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point
   291→      const conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] = [];
   292→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {
   293→        const msg = messages[i];
   294→        if (msg.role === 'user' || msg.role === 'assistant') {
   295→          conversationHistory.push({ role: msg.role, content: msg.content });
   296→        }
   297→      }
   298→
   299→      const assistantId = generateId();
   300→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
   301→
   302→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
   303→        id: generateId(),
   304→        role: 'user',
   305→        content: newContent,
   306→      };
   307→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
   308→        id: assistantId,
   309→        role: 'assistant',
   310→        content: '',
   311→        toolCalls: [],
   312→        isStreaming: true,
   313→      };
   314→
   315→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev.slice(0, messageIndex), userMsg, assistantMsg]);
   316→      setIsStreaming(true);
   317→      textBufferRef.current = '';
   318→      segmentsRef.current = [];
   319→      currentTextRef.current = '';
   320→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   321→
   322→      const controller = new AbortController();
   323→      abortRef.current = controller;
   324→
   325→      await runAgentEditLoop(
   326→        newContent,
   327→        conversationHistory,
   328→        crypto.randomUUID(),
   329→        {
   330→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   331→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
   332→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
   333→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   334→                flushText();
   335→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   336→              }, 50);
   337→            }
   338→          },
   339→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   340→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   341→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   342→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   343→            }
   344→            flushText();
   345→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   346→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   347→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   348→            }
   349→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   350→            setMessages((prev) =>
   351→              prev.map((m) =>
   352→                m.id === assistantId
   353→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   354→                  : m
   355→              )
   356→            );
   357→          },
   358→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   359→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   360→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   361→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   362→            }
   363→            flushText();
   364→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   365→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   366→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   367→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   368→            }
   369→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: pending });
   370→            setMessages((prev) =>
   371→              prev.map((m) =>
   372→                m.id === assistantId
   373→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   374→                  : m
   375→              )
   376→            );
   377→          },
   378→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   379→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   380→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   381→            );
   382→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   383→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   384→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   385→                type: 'tool',
   386→                toolResult: {
   387→                  ...pending,
   388→                  ...result,
   389→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   390→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   391→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   392→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   393→                },
   394→              };
   395→            } else {
   396→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   397→            }
   398→            setMessages((prev) =>
   399→              prev.map((m) =>
   400→                m.id === assistantId
   401→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   402→                  : m
   403→              )
   404→            );
   405→            refreshTables();
   406→          },
   407→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   408→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   409→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   410→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   411→            }
   412→            flushText();
   413→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   414→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   415→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   416→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   417→            }
   418→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   419→              type: 'subagent_start',
   420→              subagentId: data.id,
   421→              subagentName: data.name,
   422→              text: data.prompt,
   423→            });
   424→            setMessages((prev) =>
   425→              prev.map((m) =>
   426→                m.id === assistantId
   427→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   428→                  : m
   429→              )
   430→            );
   431→          },
   432→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   433→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   434→              type: 'subagent_end',
   435→              subagentId: data.id,
   436→              subagentName: data.name,
   437→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   438→              text: data.result,
   439→            });
   440→            setMessages((prev) =>
   441→              prev.map((m) =>
   442→                m.id === assistantId
   443→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   444→                  : m
   445→              )
   446→            );
   447→          },
   448→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   449→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   450→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   451→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   452→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   453→            }
   454→            flushText();
   455→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   456→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text: currentTextRef.current });
   457→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   458→            }
   459→            setMessages((prev) =>
   460→              prev.map((m) =>
   461→                m.id === assistantId
   462→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   463→                  : m
   464→              )
   465→            );
   466→            setIsStreaming(false);
   467→            abortRef.current = null;
   468→          },
   469→          onError: (error) => {
   470→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   471→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   472→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   473→            }
   474→            flushText();
   475→            setMessages((prev) =>
   476→              prev.map((m) =>
   477→                m.id === assistantId
   478→                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\n\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }
   479→                  : m
   480→              )
   481→            );
   482→            setIsStreaming(false);
   483→            abortRef.current = null;
   484→          },
   485→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   486→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   487→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   488→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   489→            }
   490→            flushText();
   491→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   492→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   493→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   494→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   495→            }
   496→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   497→              type: 'user_question',
   498→              questionData: data,
   499→            });
   500→            setMessages((prev) =>
   501→              prev.map((m) =>
   502→                m.id === assistantId
   503→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   504→                  : m
   505→              )
   506→            );
   507→          },
   508→        },
   509→        controller.signal,
   510→        userSessionId,
   511→      );
   512→    },
   513→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   514→  );
   515→
   516→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   517→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   518→      if (isStreaming) return;
   519→
   520→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   521→      setMessages((prev) => {
   522→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   523→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   524→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   525→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   526→        return remaining;
   527→      });
   528→
   529→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   530→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   531→    },
   532→    [isStreaming]
   533→  );
   534→
   535→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   536→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   537→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   538→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   539→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   540→      );
   541→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   542→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   543→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   544→          userAnswer: answers,
   545→          userFreeText: freeText,
   546→        };
   547→        setMessages((prev) =>
   548→          prev.map((m) =>
   549→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   550→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   551→              : m
   552→          )
   553→        );
   554→      }
   555→
   556→      // POST to backend
   557→      try {
   558→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   559→          method: 'POST',
   560→          headers: {
   561→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   562→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   563→          },
   564→          body: JSON.stringify({
   565→            question_id: questionId,
   566→            answers,
   567→            free_text: freeText || null,
   568→          }),
   569→        });
   570→      } catch (e) {
   571→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   572→      }
   573→    },
   574→    [userSessionId]
   575→  );
   576→
   577→  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {
   578→    if (abortRef.current) {
   579→      abortRef.current.abort();
   580→    }
   581→    setMessages([]);
   582→    setIsStreaming(false);
   583→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   584→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   585→  }, []);
   586→
   587→  return (
   588→    <AgentContext.Provider
   589→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   590→    >
   591→      {children}
   592→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   593→  );
   594→}
   595→

     1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     3→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     6→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     7→import { InlineQueryResult } from './InlineQueryResult';
     8→import { ChartWidget } from './ChartWidget';
     9→import { UserQuestion } from './UserQuestion';
    10→import './MessageBubble.css';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Strip chart_spec JSON code blocks from answer text.
    14→ * The orchestrator may include the raw chart spec JSON even though the chart
    15→ * is rendered separately. Remove ```json ... ``` blocks containing chart_spec
    16→ * or Plotly trace data so they don't appear as raw text.
    17→ */
    18→function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
    19→  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();
    20→}
    21→
    22→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder: string | undefined, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    23→  // Use streaming remainder if available
    24→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    25→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    26→    const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    27→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    28→  }
    29→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    30→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    31→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    32→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    33→      const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    34→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    35→    }
    36→  }
    37→  return t('thinking');
    38→}
    39→
    40→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isThinkingPhase, isAgentStreaming }: {
    41→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    42→  streamingRemainder?: string;
    43→  isThinkingPhase: boolean;
    44→  isAgentStreaming: boolean;
    45→}) {
    46→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    47→  // All non-answer, non-chart, non-user_question segments go inside the thinking block
    48→  const thinkingSegments = segments.filter(
    49→    (s) => s.type !== 'answer' && s.type !== 'user_question' && !(s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) && !(s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
    50→  );

   106→export function MessageBubble({ message, messageIndex }: { message: ChatMessage; messageIndex: number }) {
   107→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   108→  const { isStreaming, editMessage, deleteMessage } = useAgent();
   109→  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   110→  const [editText, setEditText] = useState(message.content);
   111→  const [isConfirmingDelete, setIsConfirmingDelete] = useState(false);
   112→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
   113→
   114→  const isUser = message.role === 'user';
   115→  const hasSegments = !isUser && message.segments && message.segments.length > 0;
   116→
   117→  useEffect(() => {
   118→    if (isEditing && textareaRef.current) {
   119→      textareaRef.current.focus();
   120→      textareaRef.current.selectionStart = textareaRef.current.value.length;
   121→    }
   122→  }, [isEditing]);
   123→
   124→  const handleEdit = () => {
   125→    setEditText(message.content);
   126→    setIsEditing(true);
   127→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   128→  };
   129→
   130→  const handleCancelEdit = () => {
   131→    setIsEditing(false);
   132→    setEditText(message.content);
   133→  };
   134→
   135→  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
   136→    const trimmed = editText.trim();
   137→    if (!trimmed || trimmed === message.content) {
   138→      handleCancelEdit();
   139→      return;
   140→    }
   141→    setIsEditing(false);
   142→    editMessage(messageIndex, trimmed);
   143→  };
   144→
   145→  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
   146→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   147→    deleteMessage(messageIndex);
   148→  };
   149→
   150→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   151→    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
   152→      handleCancelEdit();
   153→    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
   154→      e.preventDefault();
   155→      handleSaveEdit();
   156→    }
   157→  };
   158→
   159→  let streamingRemainder: string | undefined;

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Here's the issue:

1. **Backend** (`agent.py:339-340`): Subagent `text_delta` events are silently accumulated, never emitted as SSE events
2. **Backend** (`agent.py:469-489`): All text is sent as one chunk in `subagent_end`
3. **Frontend**: Receives the complete text in one event, renders it instantly

I need to change 3 files: backend emits streaming deltas, frontend handles them progressively.

> AGENT

All changes are in place. Here's a summary of what was changed:

## Changes

### 1. Backend: `backend/app/agent.py`

**Chart prompt** (line 91-99): Added data-reduction guidelines to prevent massive tool inputs.

**Subagent text streaming** (line 341): Now emits `subagent_text` SSE events for each text delta from subagents, in addition to accumulating them.

### 2. Frontend: `frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`

- Added `onSubagentText` callback to `AgentCallbacks` (line 9)
- Added `subagent_text` case to `handleSSEEvent` (lines 224-229)

### 3. Frontend: `frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`

- Added `subagentTextBufRef` and `subagentFlushRef` refs for 50ms batched flushing (same pattern as orchestrator text)
- Added `flushSubagentText()` — finds or creates `answer` segments tagged with `subagentId`, appends buffered text
- Added `onSubagentText` callback in both `sendMessage` and `editMessage` — buffers deltas, flushes every 50ms
- Updated `onSubagentEnd` — flushes remaining text, only pushes `subagent_end` segment for charts; uses streamed answer segment if available, falls back to `result` text otherwise
- Updated `onDone` and `onError` — flush subagent text buffers on stream end

**How it works**: Subagent text deltas now flow through `subagent_text` SSE events → buffered in 50ms batches → flushed into `answer` segments with `subagentId` → rendered progressively by the existing answer segment rendering in `MessageBubble`.

> DEVELOPER

what's difference with flushText and flushSubagentText

> AGENT

Good question. They serve the same purpose (batched rendering) but handle different text streams:

**`flushText`** — handles **orchestrator** text (the main agent's thinking/answer). It appends to `message.content` (a flat string), which gets rendered via `streamingRemainder` in `MessageBubble`. This is the text that appears outside of subagent blocks.

**`flushSubagentText`** — handles **subagent** text (sql-analyst/chart-builder output). It appends to `answer` **segments** tagged with a `subagentId`, since subagent text lives in the segments array, not in `message.content`.

The split exists because the two text streams have different destinations in the React state:
- Orchestrator text → `message.content` (string)
- Subagent text → `message.segments[]` (array of typed objects)

They can't share a flush function because they write to different data structures.

> DEVELOPER

> AGENT

Done. Here's what was fixed:

The SDK doesn't always yield `text_delta` stream events for subagent turns. The previous commit only emitted `subagent_text` from the `text_delta` path — which is a no-op when those events aren't received.

Now there are **two paths** that emit `subagent_text`, unified by a `subagent_turn_streamed` flag:

1. **`text_delta` path** (character-level) — used when the SDK yields stream events. Sets flag to `True`.
2. **Complete assistant message path** (per-turn) — used as fallback when `text_delta` events weren't received (`flag == False`). Resets flag for next turn.

This way, subagent answer text shows up progressively regardless of which SDK message format is available — per-character when stream events are yielded, per-turn otherwise.

Perfect! Now I have the full picture. Let me create a comprehensive report of my findings.

## Investigation Report: Full Message Flow for Subagent Turns

I've completed a thorough analysis of the message flow from the SDK to the backend and frontend. Here are my findings:

### Key Finding: The Streaming Architecture is Correct, but SDK May Not Be Delivering Intermediate Messages

**The Issue:** Subagent text appears all at once rather than streaming token-by-token.

**Root Cause Hypothesis:** The SDK (`@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk`) may not be yielding `stream_event` (SDKPartialAssistantMessage) messages for subagent turns, even though the infrastructure to handle them is in place.

---

## Detailed Analysis

### 1. SDK Message Types

The TypeScript SDK defines these critical message types (from `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/node_modules/@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk/sdk.d.ts`):

**SDKPartialAssistantMessage (stream events):**
```typescript
type SDKPartialAssistantMessage = {
    type: 'stream_event',
    event: BetaRawMessageStreamEvent,
    parent_tool_use_id: string | null,  // ← This carries the Task tool ID
    uuid: UUID,
    session_id: string,
}
```

**SDKAssistantMessage (complete messages):**
```typescript
type SDKAssistantMessage = {
    type: 'assistant',
    message: BetaMessage,
    parent_tool_use_id: string | null,  // ← Also set for subagent messages
    error?: SDKAssistantMessageError,
    uuid: UUID,
    session_id: string,
}
```

**SDKUserMessage (tool results):**
```typescript
type SDKUserMessage = {
    type: 'user',
    message: MessageParam,
    parent_tool_use_id: string | null,  // ← Set when this is a tool result from a subagent
    isSynthetic?: boolean,
    tool_use_result?: unknown,  // ← Contains the actual subagent output
    uuid?: UUID,
    session_id: string,
}
```

**Key insight:** The `tool_use_result` field on the user message contains the subagent's actual output, not just metadata.

---

### 2. Sidecar Message Forwarding

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/src/server.ts`

The sidecar simply forwards ALL SDK messages directly to the HTTP response as SSE events:

```typescript
for await (const message of sdkQuery) {
  // Reset idle timer on every message
  if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); }
  idleTimer = setTimeout(() => { ... }, SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS);
  
  if (responseEnded) break;
  // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
  res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
  // ... Langfuse tracing ...
}
```

**Critical configuration:**
```typescript
const sdkQuery = query({
  prompt: body.message,
  options: {
    // ...
    includePartialMessages: true,  // ← ENABLES stream_event emission
    // ...
  },
});
```

The sidecar **does NOT filter or transform** the `parent_tool_use_id` field. It's passed through as-is from the SDK.

---

### 3. Backend Stream Handler

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py`, `stream_chat()` function

The backend has full infrastructure to handle subagent streaming:

#### For `stream_event` messages (lines 323-363):
```python
if msg_type == "stream_event":
    event = msg.get("event", {})
    event_type = event.get("type", "")
    stream_parent = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
    is_subagent_event = bool(stream_parent and stream_parent in tool_names)
    
    if event_type == "content_block_delta":
        delta = event.get("delta", {})
        delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
        if delta_type == "text_delta":
            text = delta.get("text", "")
            if text:
                if is_subagent_event:
                    # Accumulate streaming text
                    subagent_texts[stream_parent] = subagent_texts.get(stream_parent, "") + text
                    # Emit immediately to frontend
                    yield f"event: subagent_text\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': stream_parent, 'name': tool_names.get(stream_parent, ''), 'text': text})}\n\n"
                    subagent_turn_streamed[stream_parent] = True
```

The backend **correctly identifies subagent events** by checking:
- `stream_parent` (the `parent_tool_use_id` from the SDK message) is not null
- `stream_parent` exists in `tool_names` dict (which maps Task tool IDs to subagent names like "sql-analyst" or "chart-builder")

#### For `assistant` messages (lines 366-388):
When a complete assistant message arrives with a `parent_tool_use_id`:
```python
elif msg_type == "assistant":
    message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
    parent_tool_use_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
    
    if parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in tool_names:
        # This is a subagent assistant message
        text_parts = []
        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
            if block.get("type") == "text" and block.get("text"):
                text_parts.append(block["text"])
        if text_parts:
            combined = "\n".join(text_parts)
            subagent_texts[parent_tool_use_id] = combined
            # Emit if no text_delta events were received (fallback)
            if not subagent_turn_streamed.get(parent_tool_use_id, False):
                yield f"event: subagent_text\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': parent_tool_use_id, 'name': tool_names.get(parent_tool_use_id, ''), 'text': combined})}\n\n"
```

This is a **fallback mechanism**: if the SDK doesn't yield `stream_event` messages for the subagent turn, the backend captures the complete text from the assistant message.

#### For `user` messages (lines 427-504):
When the Tool Result arrives:
```python
elif msg_type == "user":
    # Contains tool_use_result from the subagent
    tool_use_result = msg.get("tool_use_result")
    tool_use_result_text = ""
    if isinstance(tool_use_result, dict):
        tur_content = tool_use_result.get("content")
        if isinstance(tur_content, list):
            parts = []
            for item in tur_content:
                if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
                    parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
            tool_use_result_text = "\n".join(parts)
    
    # ... process tool_result blocks ...
    
    # For subagent_end (chart-builder or sql-analyst)
    if name in ("sql-analyst", "chart-builder"):
        if name == "chart-builder":
            # Try to get chart_spec from previously captured render_chart tool_use
            chart_spec = subagent_chart_specs.get(tool_id)
            end_data = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
            if chart_spec:
                end_data["chart_spec"] = chart_spec
            else:
                # Fallback to text output
                end_data["result"] = tool_use_result_text or subagent_texts.get(tool_id, text)
            yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
```

---

### 4. Frontend Event Processing

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`

The frontend correctly routes subagent events:

```typescript
case 'subagent_text':
  callbacks.onSubagentText?.({
    id: data.id as string,
    name: data.name as string,
    text: data.text as string,
  });
  break;

case 'subagent_end':
  callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
    id: data.id as string,
    name: data.name as string,
    result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
    chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
  });
  break;
```

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`

The React context handles subagent text with buffering and debouncing (50ms):

```typescript
const subagentTextBufRef = useRef<Record<string, string>>({});

const flushSubagentText = useCallback(() => {
  const bufs = subagentTextBufRef.current;
  if (Object.keys(bufs).length === 0) return;
  for (const [id, text] of Object.entries(bufs)) {
    const idx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
      (s) => s.type === 'answer' && s.subagentId === id
    );
    if (idx !== -1) {
      const seg = segmentsRef.current[idx];
      segmentsRef.current[idx] = { ...seg, text: (seg.text || '') + text };
    } else {
      segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text, subagentId: id });
    }
  }
  subagentTextBufRef.current = {};
  // Update message with new segments
  setMessages((prev) =>
    prev.map((m) =>
      m.id === assistantIdRef.current
        ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
        : m
    )
  );
}, []);

// Called with 50ms debounce
onSubagentText: (data) => {
  subagentTextBufRef.current[data.id] = (subagentTextBufRef.current[data.id] || '') + data.text;
  if (!subagentFlushRef.current) {
    subagentFlushRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      flushSubagentText();
      subagentFlushRef.current = null;
    }, 50);
  }
},
```

The frontend **batches updates** with a 50ms flush timer to avoid excessive React re-renders.

---

### 5. Message Flow Diagram

```
SDK Query Iterator
  ├─ stream_event (text_delta for Tool call)
  │   └─ Backend: Emit "tool_call" event
  │
  ├─ stream_event (text_delta for subagent turn)
  │   └─ parent_tool_use_id = "task_001" (Task tool ID)
  │   └─ Backend: Check if "task_001" in tool_names ✓
  │   └─ Backend: Emit "subagent_text" event ← STREAMING!
  │   └─ Frontend: Add text to subagentTextBufRef[task_001]
  │   └─ Frontend: Flush after 50ms debounce → Update segments
  │   └─ React: Re-render message with accumulated text
  │
  ├─ assistant (complete subagent message)
  │   └─ parent_tool_use_id = "task_001"
  │   └─ Backend: Capture text (fallback if no stream_events)
  │   └─ Backend: Extract render_chart tool_use → subagent_chart_specs
  │
  ├─ user (Tool Result)
  │   └─ parent_tool_use_id = "task_001"
  │   └─ tool_use_result.content = [{type: "text", text: "..."}]
  │   └─ Backend: Emit "subagent_end" with chart_spec or result
  │   └─ Frontend: Display chart or fallback text
  │
  └─ result (Session complete)
```

---

### 6. Critical Questions & Findings

**Q: Does `parent_tool_use_id` actually appear on stream events?**

✓ **YES** — The SDK TypeScript type definition explicitly includes it:
- `SDKPartialAssistantMessage` has `parent_tool_use_id: string | null`
- The sidecar forwards all messages as-is

**Q: Are intermediate subagent messages being yielded by the SDK?**

**UNCERTAIN** — This is the likely culprit. Possibilities:

1. **SDK not yielding stream_events for subagent turns** — The SDK may optimize away intermediate streaming for subagent calls and only yield the final `assistant` message.
2. **parent_tool_use_id not set on stream_events** — Even if stream_events are yielded, they may have `parent_tool_use_id: null`.
3. **Tool name not being registered before stream_events arrive** — If `tool_names[parent_tool_use_id]` is not yet populated when the first `stream_event` arrives, the backend will skip it.

**Q: What's the fallback mechanism?**

✓ **Works** — If stream_events aren't yielded, the backend captures the complete text from the `assistant` message (line 384-385):
```python
if not subagent_turn_streamed.get(parent_tool_use_id, False):
    # Emit complete text as single event
    yield f"event: subagent_text\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': parent_tool_use_id, ...})}\n\n"
```

This explains **why the text appears all at once** — the fallback path only emits after the complete assistant message arrives.

---

### 7. Evidence That Infrastructure Is Correct

All evidence points to the infrastructure being **properly implemented**:

1. ✓ Sidecar forwards `parent_tool_use_id` without modification (line 243)
2. ✓ Backend has complete stream_event handler (lines 323-363)
3. ✓ Backend tracks `subagent_turn_streamed[id]` to know when to use fallback (lines 293, 346)
4. ✓ Backend correctly identifies subagent events with `is_subagent_event` check (line 327)
5. ✓ Frontend correctly routes `subagent_text` events (agentService.ts line 224-229)
6. ✓ Frontend debounces/batches updates with 50ms timer (AgentContext.tsx line 228-231)
7. ✓ Tests exist for chart_spec capture from render_chart tool_use (test_stream_chart_capture.py)

---

### 8. Recommended Debugging Steps

1. **Add logging to sidecar** to see what messages are actually being yielded by the SDK:
   - Log each message type
   - Log `parent_tool_use_id` value
   - Check if `stream_event` messages have non-null `parent_tool_use_id`

2. **Add logging to backend** to trace the flow:
   - Log when `tool_names[task_id]` is populated
   - Log whether `is_subagent_event` evaluates to true
   - Log which path is taken: stream_event vs. assistant message fallback

3. **Test with a simple chart request** to verify `render_chart` tool_use is captured

4. **Check SDK version** — The behavior may have changed between SDK versions

5. **Verify `includePartialMessages: true`** is actually enabling stream_event emission

---

### 9. Conclusion

**The message flow architecture is correctly implemented end-to-end.** Subagent text streaming is designed to work via:
- `stream_event` messages with `parent_tool_use_id` → immediate "subagent_text" SSE events
- Fallback to complete `assistant` message text if stream_events aren't received

**The likely root cause of text appearing all at once is that the SDK is not yielding `stream_event` messages for subagent turns**, forcing the backend to use the fallback path which only emits after the entire assistant message is complete.

This should be verified by checking sidecar logs to see what message types are actually being emitted by the SDK iterator.

     6→from fastapi import FastAPI

    10→
    11→from app.routes import tables, query, chat, langfuse_status, config, session
    12→from app import proxy as proxy_module
    13→from app.container_manager import container_manager
    14→from app.mcp_sse import mcp_app
    15→from app.config import CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→from app.session_manager import session_manager
    20→
    21→
    22→async def _cleanup_loop():
    23→    while True:
    24→        await asyncio.sleep(60)
    25→        try:
    26→            removed = session_manager.cleanup_stale(ttl_seconds=300)
    27→            if removed:
    28→                logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d stale sessions", removed)
    29→            proxy_removed = proxy_module.proxy_token_store.cleanup_expired()
    30→            if proxy_removed:
    31→                logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d expired proxy tokens", proxy_removed)
    32→            if container_manager is not None:
    33→                container_removed = container_manager.cleanup_expired()
    34→                if container_removed:
    35→                    logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d expired containers", container_removed)
    36→        except Exception:
    37→            logger.exception("Error in background cleanup loop")
    38→
    39→
    40→@asynccontextmanager
    41→async def lifespan(app):
    42→    task = asyncio.create_task(_cleanup_loop())
    43→    yield
    44→    task.cancel()
    45→    if container_manager is not None:
    46→        container_manager.shutdown_all()
    47→
    48→
    49→app = FastAPI(title="DuckDB Data Agent API", lifespan=lifespan)
    50→
    51→app.add_middleware(
    52→    CORSMiddleware,
    53→    allow_origins=CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
    54→    allow_credentials=True,
    55→    allow_methods=["*"],
    56→    allow_headers=["*"],
    57→)
    58→
    59→app.include_router(tables.router)

    61→app.include_router(chat.router)
    62→app.include_router(langfuse_status.router)

    72→async def health():

    76→# Serve frontend static files in production
    77→STATIC_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "static"
    78→
    79→if STATIC_DIR.is_dir():
    80→    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=STATIC_DIR / "assets"), name="static-assets")
    81→
    82→    @app.get("/{full_path:path}")
    83→    async def serve_frontend(full_path: str):
    84→        """Serve frontend for any non-API route (SPA fallback)."""
    85→        file_path = (STATIC_DIR / full_path).resolve()
    86→        # Prevent path traversal: ensure resolved path is inside STATIC_DIR
    87→        if full_path and file_path.is_file() and file_path.is_relative_to(STATIC_DIR.resolve()):
    88→            return FileResponse(file_path)
    89→        return FileResponse(STATIC_DIR / "index.html")

> AGENT

Now the picture is completely clear. Every single subagent `assistant` message has:
```
block_types=['tool_use'] text_parts_count=0
```

**The subagent (Haiku) never produces text blocks** — every turn is just a `tool_use` call (execute_sql). The subagent's "answer" only arrives as one chunk in `tool_use_result.content` when it finishes entirely. There is no intermediate text to stream.

This means **character-level streaming of subagent answer text is impossible** with this SDK behavior:
1. No `stream_event` messages for subagent turns
2. No text blocks in subagent `assistant` messages — only `tool_use`
3. The answer text arrives only at the very end in `tool_use_result`

The orchestrator's post-subagent response text DOES stream (it goes through the normal `text_delta` → `event: answer` path).

Let me clean up the debug logging and revert the subagent streaming code that can never fire. The actual behavior is: subagent result text appears as one chunk (unavoidable), then orchestrator streams its response.

   551→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(

   554→
   555→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send

   557→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   558→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   559→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   560→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   561→        return remaining;
   562→      });
   563→
   564→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   565→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   566→    },
   567→    [isStreaming]
   568→  );

   570→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   571→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   572→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   573→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(

   578→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],

   584→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current

   587→          )
   588→        );
   589→      }
   590→

> AGENT

Backend is clean — only the expected `subagent_texts` dict used for the existing `subagent_end` result accumulation. No debug prints or dead `subagent_text` events.

All changes are complete:

1. **`AgentContext.tsx`**: Reverted `editMessage`'s `onSubagentEnd` to match `sendMessage` (simple push of `subagent_end` segment with `text: data.result`), removed `onSubagentText` callback, removed extra `subagentFlushRef`/`flushSubagentText` cleanup from `onDone`/`onError`, removed `flushSubagentText` from both dependency arrays.

2. **`MessageBubble.tsx`**: Removed `&& !s.subagentId` filter from `streamingRemainder` calculation since no segments with `subagentId` on `answer` type are created anymore.

3. TypeScript compiles cleanly with no errors.

The codebase is now fully reverted to the original subagent handling behavior — subagent answer text arrives as a complete chunk via the `subagent_end` SSE event and is rendered immediately (not streamed character-by-character, which was proven impossible due to SDK limitations).

+    except anyio.ClosedResourceError:

> AGENT

Committed and pushed. 

Now regarding your question about answer text not streaming for sql-analyst questions — this is the same SDK limitation we discovered:

The Claude Agent SDK **does not stream text** from subagent turns. The sql-analyst subagent's answer text only arrives as a complete chunk in `tool_use_result.content` when the subagent finishes. Every subagent assistant message contains only `tool_use` blocks (no text blocks), and no `stream_event` text deltas are emitted for subagent turns.

So the "sudden appearance" behavior is expected and cannot be fixed without changes to the SDK itself. The orchestrator's own text (thinking/answer outside of subagent calls) does stream normally — only subagent results appear all at once when the subagent completes.

Would you like to explore any workaround, such as showing a more informative loading indicator while the subagent is working?

> DEVELOPER

but why I could see streaming text showing in ui before

> AGENT

Good question. Let me investigate what's actually happening — the orchestrator's own answer text after a subagent should still stream. Let me check both the backend event emission and frontend rendering paths.

> AGENT

I found the bug. Looking at `MessageBubble.tsx`:

1. The `subagent_end` segment for sql-analyst (with `text` but no `chart_spec`) ends up in `thinkingSegments` (line 48-50)
2. But in the ThinkingBlock rendering (lines 67-94), there's **no case for `subagent_end`** — it falls through to `return null`

The sql-analyst result text is silently dropped. Before the revert, the modified `onSubagentEnd` was creating `answer` type segments for non-chart results, which DID render in the answer section. The revert restored `subagent_end` type, which has no rendering path.

The fix is to include `subagent_end` text segments in the answer section.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Committed and pushed. 

The issue was that the `subagent_end` segment type for sql-analyst (with `text` but no `chart_spec`) had no rendering path — it was included in `thinkingSegments` but the ThinkingBlock only renders `thinking`, `tool`, and `subagent_start` types. The text was silently dropped via `return null`.

The fix includes non-chart `subagent_end` segments with text in `answerSegments`, so they render in the answer section. Note that the text still won't stream character-by-character (SDK limitation) — it will appear when the subagent completes — but it will now be visible.
